([marc-aurele-besner/mymultisig-app](https://github.com/marc-aurele-besner/mymultisig-app/tree/HEAD/plugins/mymultisig-skills/skills/multisig-status) · 2 copies)
- **multisig-threshold-and-signer-rotation**: Use when configuring a Safe or similar multisig, or when rotating a signer.
